        l_text(40)     TYPE c.
  DATA: lang TYPE sy-langu.
*-- data declaration for mail log
  DATA: l_length     TYPE i,
        l_table      TYPE tsfixml,
        mail_counter TYPE int4.
  DATA: lst_formoutput  TYPE fpformoutput,
        lv_filename_out TYPE string.
  DATA lv_w_cx_root     TYPE REF TO cx_root.

*-- BAdI for email content/attachment declaration
  DATA: badi_mail      TYPE REF TO hr_in_f16_mail,
        lt_email_body  TYPE soli_tab,
        lv_length      TYPE so_obj_len,
        lv_subject     TYPE so_obj_des,
        lt_attachment  TYPE solix_tab,
        is_implemented TYPE flag.

  DATA: lv_filename3   TYPE string,
        file_length3   TYPE i,
        pdf_content3   TYPE solix_tab,
        pdf_size3      TYPE so_obj_len,
        lv_trace_file2 TYPE so_obj_des,
        lv_file        TYPE c LENGTH 1.


  CLEAR: lv_file_error,gs_log_file,p_icnum,p_tanno,is_implemented.

  CONCATENATE desc 'for ' p_ename
             INTO lv_description SEPARATED BY space.

  p_assm_start = p_endda(4).
  p_assm_end   = p_endda(4) + 1.

  rp-provide-from-last p_p0185 '02' p_begda p_endda.
  MOVE p_p0185-icnum TO p_icnum.

  rp-provide-from-last p_p0185 '05' p_begda p_endda.
  MOVE p_p0185-icnum TO p_tanno.

*  rp-read-infotype p_pernr 0105 p0105 p_begda p_endda.
  rp-provide-from-last p0105 '0010' p_begda p_endda.
  SORT p0105 DESCENDING BY endda.
  READ TABLE p0105 WITH KEY subty = '0010'.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    mailto = p0105-usrid_long.
  ENDIF.

  IF file2 IS NOT INITIAL AND year GE '2012'.
    CONCATENATE file2 p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '.PDF' INTO lv_filename1.
    CONCATENATE p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '.PDF' INTO lv_trace_file.

* upload the selected Part-A PDF to SAP GUI
    CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_upload
      EXPORTING
        filename                = lv_filename1
        filetype                = 'BIN'
        has_field_separator     = space
        header_length           = 0
        read_by_line            = 'X'
        dat_mode                = space
        codepage                = space
        ignore_cerr             = abap_true
        replacement             = '#'
      IMPORTING
        filelength              = file_length1
      CHANGING
        data_tab                = pdf_content1
      EXCEPTIONS
        file_open_error         = 1
        file_read_error         = 2
        no_batch                = 3
        gui_refuse_filetransfer = 4
        invalid_type            = 5
        no_authority            = 6
        unknown_error           = 7
        bad_data_format         = 8
        header_not_allowed      = 9
        separator_not_allowed   = 10
        header_too_long         = 11
        unknown_dp_error        = 12
        access_denied           = 13
        dp_out_of_memory        = 14
        disk_full               = 15
        dp_timeout              = 16
        not_supported_by_gui    = 17
        error_no_gui            = 18
        OTHERS                  = 19.

    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.

*     READ TABLE file_tab INTO wa_file WITH KEY pathname+26(1) = '3'.
*       IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*      CONCATENATE file3 p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '_' 'Part B' '_' '3' '.PDF' INTO lv_filename.
*         ELSE.
*     READ TABLE file_tab INTO wa_file WITH KEY pathname+26(1) = '2'.
*       IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*      CONCATENATE file3 p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '_' 'Part B' '_' '2' '.PDF' INTO lv_filename.
*         ELSE.
*     READ TABLE file_tab INTO wa_file WITH KEY pathname+26(1) = '1'.
*        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*      CONCATENATE file3 p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '_' 'Part B' '_' '1' '.PDF' INTO lv_filename.
*         ELSE.
*      CONCATENATE file3 p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '_' 'Part B' '.PDF' INTO lv_filename.
*        ENDIF.
*       ENDIF.
*      ENDIF.

      file_tab_temp[] = file_tab[].
      DELETE file_tab_temp WHERE pathname+0(10) NE p_icnum.
*    DESCRIBE TABLE file_tab_temp LINES lv_index.
*    READ TABLE file_tab_temp INTO wa_file INDEX lv_index.
      READ TABLE file_tab_temp INTO wa_file WITH KEY pathname+19(6) = 'Part B'.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0 AND form_16 IS INITIAL.
        CONCATENATE file3 wa_file-pathname INTO lv_filename.
        CONCATENATE p_icnum '_' p_assm_start '-' p_assm_end+2(2) '_'
                    'Part B' '.PDF' INTO lv_part_b.

* upload the selected Part-B PDF to SAP GUI
        CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_upload
          EXPORTING
            filename                = lv_filename
            filetype                = 'BIN'
            has_field_separator     = space
            header_length           = 0
            read_by_line            = 'X'
            dat_mode                = space
            codepage                = space
            ignore_cerr             = abap_true
            replacement             = '#'
          IMPORTING
            filelength              = file_length
          CHANGING
            data_tab                = pdf_content
          EXCEPTIONS
            file_open_error         = 1
            file_read_error         = 2
            no_batch                = 3
            gui_refuse_filetransfer = 4
            invalid_type            = 5
            no_authority            = 6
            unknown_error           = 7
            bad_data_format         = 8
            header_not_allowed      = 9
            separator_not_allowed   = 10
            header_too_long         = 11
            unknown_dp_error        = 12
            access_denied           = 13
            dp_out_of_memory        = 14
            disk_full               = 15
            dp_timeout              = 16
            not_supported_by_gui    = 17
            error_no_gui            = 18
            OTHERS                  = 19.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          IF f_path5 IS NOT INITIAL AND f10_mail IS NOT INITIAL
                                    AND year GE '2018'.
            CONCATENATE f_path5 p_icnum '_' '2f10k' '_' p_assm_start '-'
                        p_assm_end+2(2) '.PDF'
                        INTO lv_filename3.

            CONCATENATE p_icnum '_' '2f10k' '_' p_assm_start '-'
                        p_assm_end+2(2) '.PDF'
                        INTO lv_trace_file2.

            CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_upload
              EXPORTING
                filename                = lv_filename3
                filetype                = 'BIN'
                has_field_separator     = space
                header_length           = 0
                read_by_line            = 'X'
                dat_mode                = space
                codepage                = space
                ignore_cerr             = abap_true
                replacement             = '#'
              IMPORTING
                filelength              = file_length3
              CHANGING
                data_tab                = pdf_content3
              EXCEPTIONS
                file_open_error         = 1
                file_read_error         = 2
                no_batch                = 3
                gui_refuse_filetransfer = 4
                invalid_type            = 5
                no_authority            = 6
                unknown_error           = 7
                bad_data_format         = 8
                header_not_allowed      = 9
                separator_not_allowed   = 10
                header_too_long         = 11
                unknown_dp_error        = 12
                access_denied           = 13
                dp_out_of_memory        = 14
                disk_full               = 15
                dp_timeout              = 16
                not_supported_by_gui    = 17
                error_no_gui            = 18
                OTHERS                  = 19.
            IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
              attch_flag = 'X'.
              gs_log_file-emp_no   = p_pernr.
              gs_log_file-emp_mail = p0105-usrid_long.
              gs_log_file-emp_pan  = p_icnum.
              gs_log_file-filepath = lv_trace_file2.

              CONCATENATE sy-datum+6(2) sy-datum+4(2) sy-datum+0(4) INTO lv_date SEPARATED BY '-'.
              CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.
              CONCATENATE lv_date lv_time INTO gs_log_file-timestamp SEPARATED BY '/'.

              gs_log_file-status   = 'S'.
              APPEND gs_log_file TO gt_log_file.
            ELSE.
              lv_file_error = 'X'.
              gs_log_file-emp_no   = p_pernr.
              gs_log_file-emp_mail = p0105-usrid_long.
              gs_log_file-emp_pan  = p_icnum.

              CONCATENATE sy-datum+6(2) sy-datum+4(2) sy-datum+0(4) INTO lv_date SEPARATED BY '-'.
              CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.
              CONCATENATE lv_date lv_time INTO gs_log_file-timestamp SEPARATED BY '/'.

              gs_log_file-status   = 'E'.
              APPEND gs_log_file TO gt_log_file.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        IF form_16 EQ 'X'.
          IF f_path5 IS NOT INITIAL AND f10_mail IS NOT INITIAL
                                  AND year GE '2018'.
            CONCATENATE f_path5 p_icnum '_' '2f10k' '_' p_assm_start '-'
                        p_assm_end+2(2) '.PDF'
                        INTO lv_filename3.

            CONCATENATE p_icnum '_' '2f10k' '_' p_assm_start '-'
                        p_assm_end+2(2) '.PDF'
                        INTO lv_trace_file2.

            CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>gui_upload
              EXPORTING
                filename                = lv_filename3
                filetype                = 'BIN'
                has_field_separator     = space
                header_length           = 0
                read_by_line            = 'X'
                dat_mode                = space
                codepage                = space
                ignore_cerr             = abap_true
                replacement             = '#'
              IMPORTING
                filelength              = file_length3
              CHANGING
                data_tab                = pdf_content3
              EXCEPTIONS
                file_open_error         = 1
                file_read_error         = 2
                no_batch                = 3
                gui_refuse_filetransfer = 4
                invalid_type            = 5
                no_authority            = 6
                unknown_error           = 7
                bad_data_format         = 8
                header_not_allowed      = 9
                separator_not_allowed   = 10
                header_too_long         = 11
                unknown_dp_error        = 12
                access_denied           = 13
                dp_out_of_memory        = 14
                disk_full               = 15
                dp_timeout              = 16
                not_supported_by_gui    = 17
                error_no_gui            = 18
                OTHERS                  = 19.
          ENDIF.
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
            attch_flag = 'X'.
            gs_log_file-emp_no   = p_pernr.
            gs_log_file-emp_mail = p0105-usrid_long.
            gs_log_file-emp_pan  = p_icnum.
            gs_log_file-filepath = lv_trace_file2.

            CONCATENATE sy-datum+6(2) sy-datum+4(2) sy-datum+0(4) INTO lv_date SEPARATED BY '-'.
            CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.
            CONCATENATE lv_date lv_time INTO gs_log_file-timestamp SEPARATED BY '/'.

            gs_log_file-status   = 'S'.
            APPEND gs_log_file TO gt_log_file.
          ELSE.
            lv_file_error = 'X'.
            gs_log_file-emp_no   = p_pernr.
            gs_log_file-emp_mail = p0105-usrid_long.
            gs_log_file-emp_pan  = p_icnum.

            CONCATENATE sy-datum+6(2) sy-datum+4(2) sy-datum+0(4) INTO lv_date SEPARATED BY '-'.
            CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.
            CONCATENATE lv_date lv_time INTO gs_log_file-timestamp SEPARATED BY '/'.

            gs_log_file-status   = 'E'.
            APPEND gs_log_file TO gt_log_file.
          ENDIF.
        ELSE.
          lv_file_error = 'X'.
          gs_log_file-emp_no   = p_pernr.
          gs_log_file-emp_mail = p0105-usrid_long.
          gs_log_file-emp_pan  = p_icnum.

          CONCATENATE sy-datum+6(2) sy-datum+4(2) sy-datum+0(4) INTO lv_date SEPARATED BY '-'.
          CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.
          CONCATENATE lv_date lv_time INTO gs_log_file-timestamp SEPARATED BY '/'.

          gs_log_file-status   = 'E'.
          APPEND gs_log_file TO gt_log_file.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      lv_file_error = 'X'.
      gs_log_file-emp_no   = p_pernr.
      gs_log_file-emp_mail = p0105-usrid_long.
      gs_log_file-emp_pan  = p_icnum.

      CONCATENATE sy-datum+6(2) sy-datum+4(2) sy-datum+0(4) INTO lv_date SEPARATED BY '-'.
      CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.
      CONCATENATE lv_date lv_time INTO gs_log_file-timestamp SEPARATED BY '/'.

      gs_log_file-status   = 'E'.
      APPEND gs_log_file TO gt_log_file.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR p0105.
  CLEAR lt_receivers.
  REFRESH lt_receivers.

  IF NOT mailto IS INITIAL AND lv_file_error IS INITIAL.
*   send mail
    pdf_size1 = file_length1.
    pdf_size  = file_length.

    TRY.
        GET BADI badi_mail.

        is_implemented = 'X'.
*-- BAdI method call to attach a pdf document
        CALL BADI badi_mail->add_attachment
          EXPORTING
            p0001          = p0001
            file_path      = f_path
          IMPORTING
            file_length    = lv_length
            pdf_content    = lt_attachment
            attachment_sub = lv_subject.

*-- BAdI method call to change Email Content
        CALL BADI badi_mail->set_email_text
          EXPORTING
            p0001         = p0001
            panno         = p_icnum
            tanno         = final_tab-tanno
          IMPORTING
            email_body    = lt_email_body
          CHANGING
            email_subject = lv_description.

      CATCH cx_badi_not_implemented.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
*     -------- create persistent send request ------------------------
        send_request = cl_bcs=>create_persistent( ).

*     -------- create and set document -------------------------------
        document = cl_document_bcs=>create_document(
                  i_type    = 'RAW'
                  i_text    = lt_email_body
                  i_subject = lv_description ).             "#EC NOTEXT
        IF form_16 NE 'X'.
          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size
              i_attachment_subject = lv_part_b ).
        ENDIF.

*-- Attachment from BAdI
        IF lt_attachment[] IS NOT INITIAL.
          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = lt_attachment
              i_attachment_size    = lv_length
              i_attachment_subject = lv_subject ).
        ENDIF.

        IF NOT attch_flag IS INITIAL.
          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content1
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size1
              i_attachment_subject = lv_trace_file ).

          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content3
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size3
              i_attachment_subject = lv_trace_file2 ).

        ENDIF.
*     add document object to send request
        send_request->set_document( document ).
*
*      send_request->set_document( document1 ).
*     --------- add recipient (e-mail address) -----------------------
*     create recipient object
        recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address( mailto ).

*     add recipient object to send request
        send_request->add_recipient( recipient ).

        send_request->set_status_attributes( i_requested_status = 'E'  ).

*     ---------- send document ---------------------------------------
        sent_to_all = send_request->send( i_with_error_screen = 'X' ).

        COMMIT WORK.

*   ------------ exception handling ----------------------------------
*   replace this rudimentary exception handling with your own one !!!
      CATCH cx_bcs INTO bcs_exception.
        MESSAGE i865(so) WITH bcs_exception->error_type.
    ENDTRY.

  ENDIF.



ENDFORM.                    " FOLDER_FILE_TO_MAIL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_AFY_CRUN_DATE_RANGE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_YEAR  text
*      -->P_DATE_TAB  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_afy_crun_date_range  TABLES p_date_tab STRUCTURE date_range
                              USING  p_year.


  date_low+0(4)     = p_year + 1.
  date_low+4(4)     = '0301'.
  date_high+0(4)    = date_low+0(4).
  date_high+4(4)    = '0330'.
  date_range-sign   = 'E'.
  date_range-option = 'BT'.
  date_range-low    = date_low.
  date_range-high   = date_high.
  APPEND date_range TO date_tab.


ENDFORM.                    " GET_AFY_CRUN_DATE_RANGE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_FORM21BB_HEADER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FINAL_TAB  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_form21bb_header  TABLES p_final_tab STRUCTURE final_tab
                                            USING p_gs_f16data_flextxt
                                                  p_fin_start
                                                  p_fin_end.
  CLEAR gs_f12bb_header.

  CONCATENATE p_fin_start '-' p_fin_end INTO gs_f12bb_header-fyear.

  gs_f12bb_header-header = p_gs_f16data_flextxt.
  gs_f12bb_header-ename = p_final_tab-ename.
  gs_f12bb_header-addrs = p_final_tab-emp_address.
  gs_f12bb_header-eepan = p_final_tab-icnum.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_FORM12BB_HRA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P0581  text
*      -->P_FINAL_TAB_PERNR  text
*      -->P_F16_BEGDA  text
*      -->P_F16_ENDDA  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_form12bb_hra  TABLES   p_p0581 STRUCTURE p0581
                        USING    p_pernr_pernr
                                  p_f16_begda
                                  p_f16_endda.

  DATA: tmp_gt_p0581 TYPE TABLE OF p0581,
        tmp_gs_p0581 TYPE p0581.

  DATA: flag.

  DATA: amount TYPE maxbt,
        nomon  TYPE f.

  DATA: alpha(26) VALUE 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ',
        index     TYPE i.

  CLEAR: tmp_gt_p0581,
         tmp_gs_p0581,
         gs_f12bb_hra,
         gv_hra_note,
         flag.

  REFRESH: tmp_gt_p0581,
           gt_f12bb_hra.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_INFOTYPE'
    EXPORTING
*     TCLAS           = 'A'
      pernr           = p_pernr_pernr
      infty           = '0581'
      begda           = p_f16_begda
      endda           = p_f16_endda
    TABLES
      infty_tab       = tmp_gt_p0581
    EXCEPTIONS
      infty_not_found = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.

  index = 0.
  LOOP AT tmp_gt_p0581 INTO tmp_gs_p0581 WHERE
                  hrtxe EQ 1 AND accom EQ '1'.
    flag = 'X'.
*       index = sy-tabix - 1.
    gs_f12bb_hra-hradt = alpha+index(1).
    CONCATENATE gs_f12bb_hra-hradt '.' INTO gs_f12bb_hra-hradt.
    APPEND gs_f12bb_hra TO gt_f12bb_hra.
    IF p_f16_endda LT tmp_gs_p0581-endda.
      tmp_gs_p0581-endda = p_f16_endda.
    ENDIF.

    IF p_f16_begda GT tmp_gs_p0581-begda.
      tmp_gs_p0581-begda = p_f16_begda.
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION 'HR_IN_CALC_MONTHS'
      EXPORTING
        split_begda         = tmp_gs_p0581-begda
        split_endda         = tmp_gs_p0581-endda
      IMPORTING
        split_months_factor = nomon.

    amount = tmp_gs_p0581-rtamt * nomon.

    gs_f12bb_hra-hradt = '   (i) Rent paid to the landlord'.
    gs_f12bb_hra-rtamt = amount.
    CONDENSE gs_f12bb_hra-rtamt NO-GAPS.
    APPEND gs_f12bb_hra TO gt_f12bb_hra.

    CLEAR gs_f12bb_hra-rtamt.
    gs_f12bb_hra-hradt = '   (ii) Name of the landlord'.
    gs_f12bb_hra-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0581-ldnam.
    APPEND gs_f12bb_hra TO gt_f12bb_hra.

    gs_f12bb_hra-hradt = '   (iii) Address of the landlord'.
    CONCATENATE tmp_gs_p0581-ldad1 tmp_gs_p0581-ldad2 tmp_gs_p0581-ldad3
               INTO gs_f12bb_hra-evdnc SEPARATED BY ' '.
    APPEND gs_f12bb_hra TO gt_f12bb_hra.

    gs_f12bb_hra-hradt = '   (iv) PAN of the landlord'.
    gs_f12bb_hra-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0581-ldaid.
    APPEND gs_f12bb_hra TO gt_f12bb_hra.
    CLEAR gs_f12bb_hra.

    gs_f12bb_hra-hradt = '   *Additional Comments:'.
    gs_f12bb_hra-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0581-evdnc.
    APPEND gs_f12bb_hra TO gt_f12bb_hra.
    CLEAR gs_f12bb_hra.
    index = index + 1.
  ENDLOOP.

  IF flag = 'X'.
    CONCATENATE 'Note: PAN shall be furnished if'
                'the aggregate rent paid during the'
                'previous year exceeds one lakh rupees'
                 INTO gv_hra_note SEPARATED BY space.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_FORM12BB_IOS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P0584  text
*      -->P_FINAL_TAB_PERNR  text
*      -->P_F16_BEGDA  text
*      -->P_F16_ENDDA  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_form12bb_ios  TABLES   p_p0584 STRUCTURE p0584
                        USING    p_pernr_pernr
                                 p_f16_begda
                                 p_f16_endda.

  DATA: tmp_gt_p0584 TYPE TABLE OF p0584,
        tmp_gs_p0584 TYPE p0584.

  DATA: alpha(26) VALUE 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ',
        index     TYPE i.

  CLEAR: tmp_gt_p0584,
         tmp_gs_p0584,
         gs_f12bb_ios.

  REFRESH: tmp_gt_p0584,
           gt_f12bb_ios.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_SUBTYPE'
    EXPORTING
      pernr           = p_pernr_pernr
      infty           = '0584'
      subty           = '0001'
      begda           = p_f16_begda
      endda           = p_f16_endda
    TABLES
      infty_tab       = tmp_gt_p0584
    EXCEPTIONS
      infty_not_found = 1
      invalid_input   = 2
      OTHERS          = 3.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tmp_gt_p0584 INTO tmp_gs_p0584.
    IF tmp_gs_p0584-int24 IS NOT INITIAL.
      index = sy-tabix - 1.
      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = alpha+index(1).
      CONCATENATE gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt '.' INTO gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (i) Interest payable/paid to the lender'.
      gs_f12bb_ios-rtamt = tmp_gs_p0584-int24.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (ii) Name of the lender'.
      gs_f12bb_ios-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0584-lenam.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (iii) Address of the lender'.
      CONCATENATE tmp_gs_p0584-lead1 tmp_gs_p0584-lead2 tmp_gs_p0584-lead3
                    INTO gs_f12bb_ios-evdnc SEPARATED BY ' '.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (iv) PAN of the lender'.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (a) Financial Institutions(if available)'.
      IF tmp_gs_p0584-letyp EQ '(a)'.
        gs_f12bb_ios-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0584-lepan.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (b) Employer(if available)'.
      IF tmp_gs_p0584-letyp EQ '(b)'.
        gs_f12bb_ios-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0584-lepan.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.

      gs_f12bb_ios-iosdt = '   (c) Others'.
      IF tmp_gs_p0584-letyp EQ '(c)'.
        gs_f12bb_ios-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0584-lepan.
      ENDIF.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_ios TO gt_f12bb_ios.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_ios.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_FORM12BB_LTA
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P0582  text
*      -->P_FINAL_TAB_PERNR  text
*      -->P_F16_BEGDA  text
*      -->P_F16_ENDDA  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_form12bb_lta  TABLES   p_p0582 STRUCTURE p0582
                        USING    p_pernr_pernr
                                 p_f16_begda
                                 p_f16_endda.

  DATA: tmp_gt_p0582 TYPE TABLE OF p0582,
        tmp_gs_p0582 TYPE p0582.

  DATA: alpha(26) VALUE 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ',
        index     TYPE i.

  CLEAR: tmp_gt_p0582,
         tmp_gs_p0582,
         gs_f12bb_lta.

  REFRESH: tmp_gt_p0582,
           gt_f12bb_lta.

  CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_INFOTYPE'
    EXPORTING
      pernr           = p_pernr_pernr
      infty           = '0582'
      begda           = p_f16_begda
      endda           = p_f16_endda
    TABLES
      infty_tab       = tmp_gt_p0582
    EXCEPTIONS
      infty_not_found = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT tmp_gt_p0582 INTO tmp_gs_p0582.
    IF tmp_gs_p0582-subty EQ 'LTA'.
      index = sy-tabix - 1.
      gs_f12bb_lta-srlno = alpha+index(1).
      CONCATENATE gs_f12bb_lta-srlno '.' INTO gs_f12bb_lta-srlno.
      CONCATENATE ' ' gs_f12bb_lta-srlno 'Leave travel assistance:'
                      INTO gs_f12bb_lta-srlno SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_lta.

      CONCATENATE '      From' tmp_gs_p0582-stpnt 'To'
           tmp_gs_p0582-destn INTO gs_f12bb_lta-srlno SEPARATED BY space.

      gs_f12bb_lta-examt = tmp_gs_p0582-amtex.

      CONCATENATE tmp_gs_p0582-jbgdt+6(2) '/' tmp_gs_p0582-jbgdt+4(2)
                               '/' tmp_gs_p0582-jbgdt+0(4) INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc.

      CONCATENATE 'Journey Start Date:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                                SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_lta.

      CONCATENATE tmp_gs_p0582-jendt+6(2) '/' tmp_gs_p0582-jendt+4(2)
                               '/' tmp_gs_p0582-jendt+0(4) INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc.
      CONCATENATE 'Journey End Date:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                              SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.

      gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0582-stpnt.
      CONCATENATE 'Journey Start point:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                                SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.

      gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0582-destn.
      CONCATENATE 'Destination:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                         SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.

      gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0582-mtrvl.
      CONCATENATE 'Mode of travel:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                         SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.

      gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0582-ctrvl.
      CONCATENATE 'Class of Travel:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                         SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.

      gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc = tmp_gs_p0582-tktno.
      CONCATENATE 'Ticket Numbers:' gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc INTO gs_f12bb_lta-evdnc
                                                         SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_lta.
    ELSEIF tmp_gs_p0582-subty EQ 'LTC'.
      index = sy-tabix - 1.
      gs_f12bb_lta-srlno = alpha+index(1).
      CONCATENATE gs_f12bb_lta-srlno '.' INTO gs_f12bb_lta-srlno.
      CONCATENATE ' ' gs_f12bb_lta-srlno 'Leave travel concession:'
                      INTO gs_f12bb_lta-srlno SEPARATED BY space.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_lta.

      gs_f12bb_lta-examt = tmp_gs_p0582-amtex.
      APPEND gs_f12bb_lta TO gt_f12bb_lta.
      CLEAR gs_f12bb_lta.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.


ENDFORM.

  LOOP AT tmp_gt_p0585 INTO tmp_gs_p0585.

      VARYING rept_strct-sbdiv FROM tmp_gs_p0585-sbd01 NEXT tmp_gs_p0585-sbd02
      VARYING rept_strct-pcntr FROM tmp_gs_p0585-pcn01 NEXT tmp_gs_p0585-pcn02

*** Import payroll results.
  PERFORM get_rgdir TABLES rgdir USING p_pernr_pernr.

  rp-imp-c2-in.

  i = 0.

*    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.

*    IF sy-subrc NE 0.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  FILL_FORM21BB_VERIFY
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_FINAL_TAB  text
*      -->P_GS_F16DATA_FIN_START  text
*      -->P_GS_F16DATA_FIN_END  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM fill_form21bb_verify  TABLES   p_final_tab STRUCTURE final_tab
                           USING    p_pernr_pernr
                                    p_gs_f16data_fin_start
                                    p_gs_f16data_fin_end
                                    p_f16_begda
                                    p_f16_endda.
  DATA: tmp_gt_p0021 TYPE TABLE OF p0021,
        tmp_gs_p0021 TYPE p0021.

  DATA: prname       TYPE REF TO hr_in_f12bb_parent_name,
        prname_subty TYPE subty.

  gs_f12bb_verify-eenam = p_final_tab-ename.
  gs_f12bb_verify-desgn = p_final_tab-position.
  gs_f12bb_verify-place = rplace.
  gs_f12bb_verify-vdate = repodate.

  TRY.
      GET BADI prname
        FILTERS
          flt_val = '40'.
    CATCH cx_badi_not_implemented.
  ENDTRY.

** Check if BAdI is implemented or not.
** If BAdI is implemented, call the subtype returned
** from the BAdI.
  IF prname IS NOT INITIAL.
    CALL BADI prname->parent_name_subtype
      EXPORTING
        pernr = p_pernr_pernr
      CHANGING
        subty = prname_subty.

** Call the subtype 'prname_subty'
    CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_SUBTYPE'
      EXPORTING
        pernr           = p_pernr_pernr
        infty           = '0021'
        subty           = prname_subty
        begda           = '18000101'
        endda           = '99991231'
      TABLES
        infty_tab       = tmp_gt_p0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        infty_not_found = 1
        invalid_input   = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
    ENDIF.

  ELSE.
** IF BAdI is not implemented, read subtype 11.
** Fetch Fathers name
    CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_SUBTYPE'
      EXPORTING
        pernr           = p_pernr_pernr
        infty           = '0021'
        subty           = '11'
        begda           = '18000101'
        endda           = '99991231'
      TABLES
        infty_tab       = tmp_gt_p0021
      EXCEPTIONS
        infty_not_found = 1
        invalid_input   = 2
        OTHERS          = 3.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
*** Fetch mothers name if fathers name is not found
      CALL FUNCTION 'HR_READ_SUBTYPE'
        EXPORTING
          pernr           = p_pernr_pernr
          infty           = '0021'
          subty           = '12'
          begda           = '18000101'
          endda           = '99991231'
        TABLES
          infty_tab       = tmp_gt_p0021
        EXCEPTIONS
          infty_not_found = 1
          invalid_input   = 2
          OTHERS          = 3.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SORT tmp_gt_p0021 DESCENDING BY begda.
  READ TABLE tmp_gt_p0021 INTO tmp_gs_p0021 INDEX 1.
  CONCATENATE tmp_gs_p0021-favor tmp_gs_p0021-fanam INTO
                gs_f12bb_verify-prnam SEPARATED BY space.


ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F16_F12BB_FILE_TO_MAIL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P0185  text

*      -->P_PERNR_PERNR  text

*      <--P_P_ASSM_END_MAIL  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f16_f12bb_file_to_mail  TABLES   p_p0185 STRUCTURE p0185
                                      p_p0105 STRUCTURE p0105
                             USING    p_pernr
                                      desc
                                      p_ename
                                      p_begda
                                      p_endda
                             CHANGING p_assm_start
                                      p_assm_end
                                      p_fin_start
                                      p_fin_end.

  DATA: p_icnum LIKE p0185-icnum.
  DATA: p_tanno LIKE p0185-icnum.

*-- email setting's data declaration

  DATA: lt_receivers      TYPE TABLE OF somlreci1 WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA  lp_pdf_string_len TYPE i.

*-- data declaration for adding extra PDF attachment to Form 16 mail.

  DATA : lv_date       TYPE string.
  DATA : lv_time       TYPE string.
  DATA : lv_filename   TYPE string.
  DATA : lv_filename1  TYPE string.
  DATA : lv_filename2  TYPE string.
  DATA : lv_trace_file TYPE so_obj_des.
  DATA : lv_trace_file1 TYPE so_obj_des.
  DATA : lv_part_b     TYPE so_obj_des.
  DATA : lv_file_error TYPE flag.

*-- data declaration for uploading PDF
  DATA  file_length   TYPE i.
  DATA  file_length1  TYPE i.
  DATA  file_length2  TYPE i.
  DATA  filename      TYPE string.
  DATA  filename1     TYPE string.
  DATA  file_tab_temp TYPE TABLE OF sdokpath.
  DATA  lv_index      TYPE i.

*-- data declaration for sending via BCS
  DATA send_request  TYPE REF TO cl_bcs.
  DATA document      TYPE REF TO cl_document_bcs.
  DATA recipient     TYPE REF TO if_recipient_bcs.
  DATA bcs_exception TYPE REF TO cx_bcs.
  DATA sent_to_all   TYPE os_boolean.
  DATA pdf_size      TYPE so_obj_len.
  DATA pdf_size1     TYPE so_obj_len.
  DATA pdf_size2     TYPE so_obj_len.
  DATA pdf_content   TYPE solix_tab.
  DATA pdf_content1  TYPE solix_tab.
  DATA pdf_content2  TYPE solix_tab.
  DATA pdf_xstring   TYPE xstring.
  DATA pdf_xstring1  TYPE xstring.
  DATA pdf_xstring12  TYPE xstring.
  DATA: mailto       TYPE ad_smtpadr.
  DATA: attch_flag   TYPE flag.
  DATA: lfd_partb_counter TYPE flag.

*-- get document data
  DATA: lv_description TYPE so_obj_des,
        l_text(40)     TYPE c.
  DATA: lang TYPE sy-langu.
*-- data declaration for mail log
  DATA: l_length     TYPE i,
        l_table      TYPE tsfixml,
        mail_counter TYPE int4.
  DATA: lst_formoutput  TYPE fpformoutput,
        lv_filename_out TYPE string.
  DATA lv_w_cx_root     TYPE REF TO cx_root.

*-- BAdI for email content/attachment declaration
  DATA: badi_mail      TYPE REF TO hr_in_f16_mail,
        lt_email_body  TYPE soli_tab,
        lv_length      TYPE so_obj_len,
        lv_subject     TYPE so_obj_des,
        lt_attachment  TYPE solix_tab,
        is_implemented TYPE flag.

  DATA: lv_filename3   TYPE string,
        file_length3   TYPE i,
        pdf_content3   TYPE solix_tab,
        pdf_size3      TYPE so_obj_len,
        lv_trace_file2 TYPE so_obj_des,
        lv_file        TYPE c LENGTH 1.

  CLEAR: lv_file_error,gs_log_file,p_icnum,p_tanno,is_implemented.

             INTO lv_description SEPARATED BY space.

  p_assm_end   = p_endda(4) + 1.

  p_fin_end   = p_begda(4) + 1.

  MOVE p_p0185-icnum TO p_icnum.

  rp-provide-from-last p_p0185 '05' p_begda p_endda.

            CONCATENATE sy-uzeit+0(2) sy-uzeit+2(2) sy-uzeit+4(2) INTO lv_time SEPARATED BY ':'.

    pdf_size1 = file_length1.
    pdf_size  = file_length.
    pdf_size3 = file_length3.

    TRY.
        GET BADI badi_mail.

        is_implemented = 'X'.
*-- BAdI method call to attach a pdf document
        CALL BADI badi_mail->add_attachment
          EXPORTING
            p0001          = p0001
            file_path      = f_path
          IMPORTING
            file_length    = lv_length
            pdf_content    = lt_attachment
            attachment_sub = lv_subject.

*-- BAdI method call to change Email Content

          EXPORTING
            p0001         = p0001
            panno         = p_icnum
            tanno         = final_tab-tanno
          IMPORTING
            email_body    = lt_email_body
          CHANGING
            email_subject = lv_description.

      CATCH cx_badi_not_implemented.
    ENDTRY.

    TRY.
*     -------- create persistent send request ------------------------
        send_request = cl_bcs=>create_persistent( ).

*     -------- create and set document -------------------------------
        document = cl_document_bcs=>create_document(
                  i_type    = 'RAW'
                  i_text    = lt_email_body
                  i_subject = lv_description ).             "#EC NOTEXT
        IF form_16 NE 'X'.
          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size
              i_attachment_subject = lv_part_b ).
        ENDIF.

*-- Attachment from BAdI
        IF lt_attachment[] IS NOT INITIAL.
          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = lt_attachment
              i_attachment_size    = lv_length
              i_attachment_subject = lv_subject ).
        ENDIF.

        IF NOT attch_flag IS INITIAL.
          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content1
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size1
              i_attachment_subject = lv_trace_file ).

          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content2
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size2
              i_attachment_subject = lv_trace_file1 ).

          CALL METHOD document->add_attachment(
              i_attachment_type    = 'PDF'
              i_att_content_hex    = pdf_content3
              i_attachment_size    = pdf_size3
              i_attachment_subject = lv_trace_file2 ).


        ENDIF.
*     add document object to send request
        send_request->set_document( document ).
*
*      send_request->set_document( document1 ).
*     --------- add recipient (e-mail address) -----------------------
*     create recipient object
        recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address( mailto ).

*     add recipient object to send request
        send_request->add_recipient( recipient ).

        send_request->set_status_attributes( i_requested_status = 'E'  ).

*     ---------- send document ---------------------------------------
        sent_to_all = send_request->send( i_with_error_screen = 'X' ).

        COMMIT WORK.

*   ------------ exception handling ----------------------------------
*   replace this rudimentary exception handling with your own one !!!
      CATCH cx_bcs INTO bcs_exception.
        MESSAGE i865(so) WITH bcs_exception->error_type.
    ENDTRY.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  DISPLAY_ALV_12BB
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM display_alv_12bb .
  TYPE-POOLS: slis.
  DATA: fieldcat TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv WITH HEADER LINE,
        BEGIN OF g_itab_fcode OCCURS 10,
          fcode LIKE rsmpe-func,
        END OF g_itab_fcode.

  CLEAR  fieldcat.
*Field catalog for Form 12BB mail log

  fieldcat-fieldname   = 'PERNR'.
  fieldcat-seltext_m   = 'Employee Number'.
  fieldcat-outputlen = 15.
  APPEND fieldcat TO fieldcat.
  CLEAR  fieldcat.

  fieldcat-fieldname   = 'ERROR'.
  fieldcat-seltext_l   = 'Error Detail'.
  fieldcat-outputlen = 50.
  APPEND fieldcat TO fieldcat.
  CLEAR  fieldcat.

  REFRESH g_itab_fcode.
  MOVE 'AMBC' TO g_itab_fcode-fcode.
  APPEND g_itab_fcode.
  MOVE 'CORC' TO g_itab_fcode-fcode.
  APPEND g_itab_fcode.
  MOVE 'PERR' TO g_itab_fcode-fcode.
  APPEND g_itab_fcode.
  MOVE 'PFRM' TO g_itab_fcode-fcode.
  APPEND g_itab_fcode.

  PERFORM display_alv_grid IN PROGRAM hincalv0
                         TABLES gt_f12bb_mail_log fieldcat g_itab_fcode
                         USING sy-repid 'Form 12BB' 'Error log details'.

ENDFORM.
